# RECENT_WINS  (L3M = 2025-11-06..2026-02-06, 13 weeks; own capped mask; compiled 2026-10-31 09:17Z)

**This table is a VIEW, not a verdict.** Nothing is promoted by being in it; the tier column carries the gates. Rows are cells that were POSITIVE in the last 3 months on the primary measure. They were not selected on L3M, but L3M has been looked at for every one of them, so the L3M numbers are the most-looked-at window we have. Rows from a program with tens of thousands of cells are what noise looks like (see the multiplicity column); "$ at live lots" are not additive across rows (many rows are the same trade stream, cells of one program overlap, and units differ by program: stack-level lock-engine deltas vs per-fill 1-lot approximations, see each note). Closed programs are RE-READ from stored windows / per-trade or per-day series (no re-run, no new selection).

## RECENT-EDGE ledger (T-RECENT-EDGE; this page is a view; a recent edge's value is its shrunk expectation, not its L3M number)

Ledger = rows positive in L3M AND L6M on the primary measure after de-duplication: **14741 rows** (10744 further positive-L3M rows have no L6M value and are listed only in the older view below). **Status counts:** NEW 5390, RISING 3549, STABLE 1566, FADING 4236, STOPPED 0. **Implemented in D6 / THP_POLICY:** 24 of 14741 rows (on the Sunday ON list or a live THP_POLICY line); 186 on the shadow list; the rest neither.

(transitions since the last compile; 0 in all, see EDGE_STATUS_LOG.md)

How to read the new columns: PERSIST = historical probability that a rule with this family / magnitude (L3M and L6M t-statistic proxy) / PRE sign stays positive over the NEXT 3 months (walk-forward model of DS3_PERSISTENCE.md; for most families it is below 50% because the typical cell has a negative long-run mean; compare with the noise baseline in that report). AGE = consecutive positive months, from a stored monthly series where one exists for the row, else blank. DRIFT = the six calendar-month values 2025-08..2026-01 in the unit named in the report (per base trade or stack $ per week), only for rows whose cell has a stored per-trade/per-day series (CF-ENG, EINT1, UF-LOSS, L2SJ stack lines). HALF-LIFE = family decay of the selected edge from month +1 to +6 (months). SHRUNK L3M = family OOS/IS ratio x L3M. SIZE = uncertainty-adjusted Kelly fraction of normal size, mu_s^2 / (mu_s^2 + SE^2 + (mu x se_shrink)^2) with mu_s the shrunk expectation, capped at 1.0, 0 if mu_s <= 0 (SE = MDE/2.8). STATUS: NEW (turned on within L3M or first month), RISING / STABLE / FADING (L3M vs the earlier 3 months), STOPPED from the RE-2 monitor. REGIME = book/session carrying the row; the day-vol tercile split is not stored per row (see DS3_PERSISTENCE.md). IMPLEMENTED = on the Sunday ON list / live THP_POLICY line / shadow list / neither.
